530	    // 入水溅落声（与主角一致）：音量随与主角距离衰减，Boss 更响
531	    const eli = game.world.store.liquid[game.world.store.idx(
532	      Math.floor(this.cx / TILE), Math.floor(this.cy / TILE),
533	    )];
534	    const wasWater = this.inWater;
535	    this.inWater = eli > 80;
536	    if (this.inWater && !wasWater && hasPlayer) {
537	      const d = Math.hypot(player.cx - this.cx, player.cy - this.cy);
538	      const vol = Math.max(0.12, Math.min(1, 1 - d / (TILE * 45))) * (this.def.boss ? 1.2 : 0.9);
539	      game.playSfx('splash', vol);
540	    }
541	
542	    // 岩浆接触（NPC.cs:94520-94545）：30t 独立冷却（immune[255],不受普通受击 iframes 影响）,
543	    // onFire 7s + 50 直伤;lavaImmune 族（NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE,SetDefaults 提取）完全豁免
544	    if (this.vanillaId === null || !NPC_LAVA_IMMUNE.has(this.vanillaId)) {
545	      if (this.lavaCd > 0) this.lavaCd--;
546	      const inLava = liquidCollision(game.world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, 2);
547	      if (inLava) {
548	        this.lavaWet = true;
549	        if (this.lavaCd <= 0) {
550	          this.lavaCd = 30;
551	          this.onFireT = Math.max(this.onFireT, 7); // AddBuff(24,420)
552	          this.hurt(50, 0, 0, game);                // StrikeNPCNoInteraction(50)
553	          game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#FF9A3C', 10, 1.6, { life: 30, grav: -0.05 });
554	        }
555	      } else {
556	        this.lavaWet = false;
557	      }
558	    } else {
559	      this.lavaWet = false;
560	    }
561	    // 入水熄灭 onFire（NPC.cs:94284 TryRemovingWaterPerishableEffects:水湿(非岩浆)→DelBuff 24）
562	    if (this.inWater && !this.lavaWet) this.onFireT = 0;
563	    // onFire DoT（NPC.cs:92619:lifeRegen-8 = 4 HP/s,无防御结算）+ 火尘粒子（:92187）
564	    if (this.onFireT > 0) {
565	      this.onFireT -= dt;
566	      this.onFireDoT += 4 * dt;
567	      if (this.onFireDoT >= 1) {
568	        const n = Math.floor(this.onFireDoT);
569	        this.onFireDoT -= n;
570	        this.hp -= n;
571	        this.hpBarT = 240;
572	        if (this.hp <= 0) this.hurt(9999, 0, 0, game); // 走标准死亡管线(掉落/分裂/联机)
573	      }
574	      if (Math.random() < 0.75) {
575	        game.spawnParticles(
576	          this.x + Math.random() * this.w, this.y + Math.random() * this.h,
577	          Math.random() < 0.7 ? '#FF9A3C' : '#FFD24A', 1, 0.8,
578	          { life: 26 + Math.random() * 14, grav: -0.06, damp: 0.96 });
579	      }
580	    }

582	    // ---- 武器浸剂 debuff 滴血（R2，NPC.cs:92585/92697/92708 DoT 同 onFire 模式） ----
583	    if (this.poisonT > 0) { this.poisonT -= dt; this.poisonDoT += 6 * dt; }
584	    if (this.venomT > 0) { this.venomT -= dt; this.venomDoT += 30 * dt; }
585	    if (this.cursedT > 0) { this.cursedT -= dt; this.cursedDoT += 24 * dt; }
586	    for (const [tKey, dKey] of [['poisonT', 'poisonDoT'], ['venomT', 'venomDoT'], ['cursedT', 'cursedDoT']] as const) {
587	      const acc = this[dKey];
588	      if (acc >= 1) {
589	        const n = Math.floor(acc);
590	        this[dKey] -= n;
591	        this.hp -= n;
592	        this.hpBarT = 240;
593	        if (this.hp <= 0) this.hurt(9999, 0, 0, game);
594	      }
595	    }
596	    if (this.ichorT > 0) this.ichorT -= dt;
597	    if (this.midasT > 0) this.midasT -= dt;
598	    if (this.confusedT > 0) {
599	      this.confusedT -= dt;
600	      // 纳米（confused 近似）：每 60t 翻转水平速度方向（原版 AI 反向 :29811）
601	      this.confuseFlip += dt;
602	      if (this.confuseFlip >= 1) { this.confuseFlip = 0; this.vx *= -1; }
603	    }

605	    // 接触伤害（带上攻击者引用，供荆棘反伤）。damage=0（小动物等友好生物）不触发——

607	    if (hasPlayer && this.aabbOverlaps(player) && this.def.damage > 0) {

611	    // Despawn 系统（原版 CheckActive, NPC.cs:78669-78798 + EncourageDespawn :7228-7235）：
612	    // timeLeft(activeTime=750) 屏内矩形每 tick 重置+清鼓励旗；离屏从当前值倒数，归零即消失。
613	    // EncourageDespawn(10) 封顶后离屏 ~10t 即消；屏内每 tick 被重置抵挡——
614	    // 僵尸/眼球白天游荡或上飞，一离屏就消失（原版天亮清夜怪的实际语义）。
615	    // Boss 默认豁免，但 despawnEncouraged（骷髅王离场/原版通用 EncourageDespawn）后
616	    // 同样倒数——且玩家死亡（player=null）时视为离屏（原版 CheckActive 不依赖玩家存活）
617	    if (this.vanillaId === 488 || (this.vanillaId != null && this.vanillaId in TOWER_KEY)

12	| 塔位置 | 4 个**等距列**：x = maxTilesX/5 ×(1+j)（j=0..3），每列 x 抖动 ±100 格；自 worldSurface 向下找首个非实心窗口（x±10 格 / 上 20 下 15 格净空）；30 次尝试失败兜底 (列x, worldSurface-40)。四塔 id 洗牌分配 | WorldGen.cs:87371-87436 |

15	| 扣盾 | 本组专属怪死亡 → 发射 projectile 629 TowerDamageBolt（aiStyle 122，5px/t 追塔、红尘尾），命中塔：盾 -1、塔 ai[3]=1（闪光 120t） | 发弹 NPC.cs:80080-80121；命中 Projectile.cs:69783-69819 |
16	| 塔血尽 | StrikeNPC：ai[2]≠1 时 → ai[2]=1、ai[1]=0、life 回满并无敌，进入 180t 上升渐隐演出后才真死 | NPC.cs:78864-78873；演出 NPC.cs:41030-41133 |
17	| 塔真死 | downedTower_X=true、TowerActive_X=false、`UpdateLunarApocalypse()` + `MessageLunarApocalypse()`，并走常规 NPCLoot（碎片） | NPC.cs:80122-80146 |
18	| 四塔全灭 | UpdateLunarApocalypse：场上无 517/422/507/493 且无 398 → `StartImpendingDoom(3600)`：LunarApocalypseIsUp=false、MoonLordCountdown=Max=3600（60s）、播 Lang.misc[52]、清教徒 | WorldGen.cs:87438-87503 |
19	| 月总降临 | 每帧倒计时 -1，归零 → `NPC.SpawnOnPlayer(最近玩家, 398)`；期间 MoonLordShake 震屏滤镜；BGM 强制 38 | Main.cs:64436-64459 |
20	| 公告 | 每倒一塔播 `Lang.misc[43+已倒数]`：43 天界入侵 / 44 头脑麻木 / 45 痛苦 / 46 阴森低语（47 需 num=4 不可达） | WorldGen.cs:87523-87550 |

22	| 持久化 | 存 downedTower_*、TowerActive_*、LunarApocalypseIsUp；**盾值不存**（读档 TowerActive=true 重置满盾） | WorldFile.cs:1352-1360 / 2220-2245 |

27	
28	四塔 SetDefaults 一致：lifeMax 20000 / def 20 / dmg 0 / 130×270 / noGravity / noTileCollide / kbResist 0 / **npcSlots 0**（不占刷怪槽）。

35	5. 悬停：velocity.Y = sin(2π·ai[0]/300)·0.5；ai[0] 满 300 归零。贴地：底部向下 10/20/30 格探测，近下沉 1.5、远上浮。

// 弹幕命中目标:怪物桶之外还有小动物桶(critters)。
// 原版小动物是 NPC(NPCID.Squirrel 等),任何伤害即死——近战挥砍已覆盖
// (Game 挥砍段"小动物:任何挥击一击致死"),弹幕此前漏判,箭射松鼠无效果。
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import { BuffType } from '../stats/Buffs';

export interface CritterLike {
  x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number;
  dead: boolean;
  /** 小动物受击:一击致死,返回是否生效(iframes 内 false) */
  hurt(game: GameHooks): boolean;
}

/** 弹幕与实体 AABB 重叠 */
export function projOverlap(ax: number, ay: number, aw: number, ah: number, e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }): boolean {
  return ax < e.x + e.w && ax + aw > e.x && ay < e.y + e.h && ay + ah > e.y;
}

/** 命中小动物:一击致死。返回是否命中(调用方按各自语义消耗穿透/销毁弹体) */
export function hitCritters(self: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, game: GameHooks): boolean {
  const list = (game as unknown as { critters?: () => unknown[] }).critters?.() ?? [];
  for (const ent of list) {
    const c = ent as CritterLike;
    if (c.dead) continue;
    if (projOverlap(self.x, self.y, self.w, self.h, c)) {
      c.hurt(game);
      return true;
    }
  }
  return false;
}

/** 弹幕命中怪物:播受击音效(原版 NPC.StrikeNPC 语义——任意伤害来源都响,
 *  近战挥砍早已如此;弹幕此前漏了) */
export function playEnemyHitSound(game: GameHooks, e: { def?: { hitSound?: string[] } }): void {
  const files = e.def?.hitSound;
  if (files && files.length) game.playSfxFiles(files, 0.9);
}

/** 敌对弹幕命中玩家(原版 Projectile.Damage_EVP,Projectile.cs:13706-13830):
 *  - 门禁 hostile + damage>0(:13708)——调用方用 hostile 标志控制,本函数只做判定;
 *  - 玩家 dead / 无敌即跳过(:13732 player.immune)——Player.damage 自带 iframes,
 *    与原版 immune 同义去重,无需调用方另记冷却;
 *  - 相交判定 = AABB 重叠(Colliding :13749,Rect.Intersects 同构);
 *  - 伤害结算走 GameHooks.damagePlayer(= Player.Hurt 入口,含防御减算/击退/
 *    受击音/伤害数字/饰品触发)。
 *  原版敌弹命中【不消耗穿透】(:13811-13829 仅 435/436/437/681/682 特判递减),
 *  弹体照常飞行——调用方不得据此销毁弹体。
 *  返回是否【实际造成伤害】(iframes/闪避吞掉时 false,对应原版 Hurt 返回 0;
 *  StatusPlayer 的 debuff 授予以此为准,:13798-13800)。 */

OK, there's no generic vanilla projectile system. In the ai[1]==1 branch (the death/escape sequence): `if (this.ai[1] == 1f) { localAI[3]==0 → set ai[3]=3, countdown 3 ticks then active=false }` — that's the despawn sequence when the target player is dead: `ai[0]=0, ai[1]=1, ai[2]=0, ai[3]=0`.

So ai[1]=20 after teleport... unused elsewhere? localAI[1] != ai[1]. Hmm, ai[1]=20 might be legacy. Fine.

- Projectile 574: follow floater 180 ticks (velocity += sign(delta)*(0.15,0.075), max 6, position follows NPC movement), then fly toward player and shoot 576 projectile (damage 65) if line of sight; explode on kill (Kill() at 69366 only covers 539). For 574 kill: probably normal. And the NPC teleport check `projectile10.ai[0] >= 0f` (returning state negative).

This is getting deep. For our codebase, do we have a generic "vanilla projectile" entity? We have Dart, MagicProj, WeaponProj... The doc grants simplification licenses only for 540/578/579 portals.

Given scope, a reasonable 1:1-with-registered-dependency approach for 574: implement it as a lightweight dedicated projectile class or fold into existing. 123	
124	export class TrapShot extends Entity {
125	  w = 8; h = 8;
126	  vx: number;
127	  vy: number;
128	  damage: number;
129	  kind: TrapShotKind;
130	  /** 传送门弹 601 的 ai[0]（WorldGen.cs:50622-50631：0 橙门 / 1 蓝门）；
131	   *  烟花族 = 色变体（167-170 / 415-418 / 419-422 减基址） */
132	  form = 0;
133	  /** 喷泉弹锚点 tile（Projectile.ai[0]/ai[1]，Wiring.cs:1548；锚点 338 消失即 Kill） */
134	  ax = -1;
135	  ay = -1;
136	  /** 滚转角(尖刺球/巨石/炮弹族) */
137	  private rot = 0;
138	  /** 已飞行 tick（炮弹族重力延迟用，对应原版 ai[0]） */
139	  private age = 0;
140	  life = 600;
141	  dead = false;
142	
143	  /** 全透明贴图缓存(xnb 解包异常的 187/654 等):检出一次后走色块回退,避免隐形弹幕 */
144	  private static blankTex = new Set<string>();
145	  private static okTex = new Set<string>();
146	  private static isBlank(r: Renderer, path: string): boolean {
147	    if (TrapShot.blankTex.has(path)) return true;
148	    if (TrapShot.okTex.has(path)) return false;
149	    const img = r.atlas?.vimages.get(path);
150	    let blank = true;
151	    if (img) {
152	      const c = document.createElement('canvas');
153	      c.width = img.width; c.height = img.height;
154	      const cx = c.getContext('2d')!;
155	      cx.drawImage(img, 0, 0);
156	      const d = cx.getImageData(0, 0, c.width, c.height).data;
157	      for (let i = 3; i < d.length; i += 4) { if (d[i] > 30) { blank = false; break; } }
158	    }
159	    (blank ? TrapShot.blankTex : TrapShot.okTex).add(path);
160	    return blank;
161	  }
162	
163	  constructor(x: number, y: number, vx: number, vy: number, damage: number, kind: TrapShotKind,
164	    form = 0, anchor?: [number, number]) {
165	    super();
166	    const s = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[kind];
167	    this.x = x; this.y = y;
168	    this.vx = vx; this.vy = vy;
169	    this.damage = damage;
170	    this.kind = kind;
171	    this.form = form;
172	    if (anchor) { this.ax = anchor[0]; this.ay = anchor[1]; }
173	    this.w = s.w; this.h = s.h;
174	    if (kind === 'spiky') this.life = 1800; // 原版尖刺球 timeLeft 1800
175	    if (s.life !== undefined) this.life = s.life; // 炮弹族 timeLeft（SetDefaults 默认 3600 / 281=600）
176	  }
177	
178	  draw(r: Renderer, _cam: Camera): void {
179	    // 世界坐标绘制(Renderer 实体循环运行在世界变换内,勿再自算屏幕坐标)
180	    const ctx = r.canvas.getContext('2d');
181	    if (!ctx) return;
182	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
183	    if (st.invisible) return; // 733 alpha=255：隐形纯发射器（SetDefaults :7440）
184	    // 烟花族按 form 选色变体贴图；单贴图 kind 走 proj
185	    const tex = st.projs?.[this.form] ?? st.proj;
186	    const img = tex && r.atlas ? r.atlas.vimages.get(tex) : null;
187	    if (img && st.fw && st.fh && !TrapShot.isBlank(r, tex!)) {
188	      // 原版弹幕贴图(整帧):飞行类贴图默认朝上(镖尖/火苗向上),对齐速度方向需 +90°;
189	      // 尖刺球/巨石不随速度旋转,按位移滚转
190	      ctx.save();
191	      ctx.translate(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
192	      if (st.spin) ctx.rotate(this.rot);
193	      else if (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy) > 0.01) ctx.rotate(Math.atan2(this.vy, this.vx) + (st.rotOff ?? 0));
194	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, st.fw, st.fh, -st.fw / 2, -st.fh / 2, st.fw, st.fh);
195	      ctx.restore();
196	      return;
197	    }
198	    ctx.fillStyle = st.color;
199	    ctx.fillRect(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h);
200	  }
201	
202	  fixedUpdate(_dt: number, game: GameHooks) {
203	    // 联机傀儡（远端弹幕快照驱动）：纯位移外推，不跑命中/碰撞（伤害归拥有者客户端结算）
204	    if (this.netPuppet) { this.netPuppetStep(); return; }
205	    // 烟花三件套 + 马桶水花：独立 AI 族（aiStyle 34/73/153），不走陷阱弹通用位移/命中链
206	    if (this.kind === 'fireworkRocket' || this.kind === 'fireworksBoxRocket'
207	      || this.kind === 'fireworkFountain' || this.kind === 'toiletPop') {
208	      this.fireworkUpdate(game);
209	      return;
210	    }
211	    if (--this.life <= 0) {
212	      // 炮弹族 timeLeft 耗尽同样走 Kill 爆炸（Projectile.Kill 由 timeLeft 归零触发）
213	      if (TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind].blast) this.blastKill(game);
214	      else this.dead = true;
215	      return;
216	    }
217	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
218	    this.age++;
219	    // 首帧发射音（Projectile.AI：162=Projectile.cs:21542-21546 / 281=26830-26833 SoundID.Item14）
220	    if (this.age === 1 && st.launchSfx) game.playSfx(st.launchSfx, 1, this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2);
221	    // 滚转：尖刺球/巨石近似 | 炮弹族按原版
222	    if (st.spin === true) this.rot += this.vx * 0.03;
223	    else if (st.spin === 'roll') {
224	      // aiStyle2（Projectile.cs:21528）：rotation += (|vx|+|vy|)*0.03*direction
225	      this.rot += (Math.abs(this.vx) + Math.abs(this.vy)) * 0.03 * (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1);
226	    } else if (st.spin === 'bunny') {
227	      // aiStyle49（Projectile.cs:26841）：rotation += vx*0.05 + direction*0.05
228	      this.rot += this.vx * 0.05 + (this.vx < 0 ? -1 : 1) * 0.05;
229	    }
230	    if (st.gravDelay !== undefined) {
231	      // 炮弹族弧线：前 gravDelay tick 直线，之后 vy+=grav / vx*=xDamp
232	      // （162/281=Projectile.cs:21546-21550/26844-26849 的 ai[0]>=18；166=:21879-21884 的 >=20）
233	      if (this.age >= st.gravDelay) {
234	        this.vy += st.grav;
235	        this.vx *= st.xDamp ?? 1;
236	        if (st.vCap !== undefined && this.vy > st.vCap) this.vy = st.vCap; // 281 :26850-26853
237	      }
238	    } else {
239	      this.vy += st.grav;
240	    }
241	    this.x += this.vx;
242	    this.y += this.vy;
243	    // 粒子拖尾(原版火焰陷阱/热喷泉的视觉主体是沿途粒子;每 2 tick 一次防刷屏)
244	    if (st.trail && (this.life & 1) === 0) {
245	      game.spawnParticles(
246	        this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2,
247	        st.trail.colors[(this.life >> 1) % st.trail.colors.length],
248	        st.trail.count, 0.4, { life: st.trail.life, damp: 0.96, grav: st.trail.grav },
249	      );
250	    }
251	    // 传送门弹拖尾（Projectile.cs:51180-51192：尘 264 = 门色，无重力）
252	    if (this.kind === 'portalbolt' && (this.life & 1) === 0) {
253	      game.spawnParticles(
254	        this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2,
255	        this.form === 0 ? '#FFAA28' : '#3CE0FF', 1, 0.6, { life: 14, damp: 0.94, grav: 0 },
256	      );
257	    }
258	    const tx = Math.floor((this.x + this.w / 2) / TILE);
259	    const ty = Math.floor((this.y + this.h / 2) / TILE);
260	    if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) {
261	      // 传送门弹撞块（Projectile.cs:16672-16686）：落门后消亡（音效 Item115/114 按 form）
262	      if (this.kind === 'portalbolt') {
263	        game.placePortal?.(this.x, this.y, this.vx, this.vy, this.form);
264	        game.playSfx(this.form === 0 ? 'portalOrange' : 'portalBlue', 1, this.x, this.y);
265	        this.dead = true;
266	        return;
267	      }
268	      // 尖刺球/巨石:撞块反弹衰减
269	      if (this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'boulder') {
270	        if (Math.abs(this.vy) > 1) { this.vy *= -0.4; this.vx *= 0.7; this.y -= this.vy; }
271	        else { this.vy = 0; this.vx *= 0.94; }
272	      } else if (st.blast) {
273	        // 炮弹/兔兔炮撞块 → Kill 落点爆炸（Projectile.cs:72768-72831 / 72704-72758）
274	        this.blastKill(game);
275	        return;
276	      } else {
277	        // 雪球撞块碎裂（Projectile.cs:71758-71767）
278	        if (st.shatterDust) this.shatter(game);
279	        this.dead = true;
280	        return;
281	      }
282	    }
283	    // 友方弹幕（炮弹族 friendly=true）：不伤玩家，直接进敌怪判定。
284	    // 敌对陷阱：伤害走 damagePlayer(防御减算/受击音/iframes 去重);除巨石外命中即碎
285	    if (!st.friendly) {
286	      if (projOverlap(this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, game.player) && !game.player.dead) {
287	        hitPlayer(this, game, this.damage);
288	        if (this.kind !== 'boulder') this.dead = true;
289	        if (this.dead) return;
290	      }
291	    }
292	
293	    // ---- 命中怪物/小动物/城镇 NPC（原版陷阱弹幕对全实体敌对，Projectile.DamageNPC 语义） ----
294	    // 穿透规则（近似原版）：巨石碾压穿透；尖刺球/火焰穿透（靠受击者无敌帧节流连击）；
295	    // 镖/超级飞镖/热喷泉一碰即碎（与对玩家一致）；
296	    // 炮弹族：162 penetrate4 / 281 penetrate-1（SetDefaults）→ 穿透，撞块/超时才 Kill 爆炸；
297	    // 166 雪球 penetrate1 → 一碰即碎
298	    const pierce = this.kind === 'boulder' || this.kind === 'spiky' || this.kind === 'flame'
299	      || this.kind === 'cannonball' || this.kind === 'bunny';
300	    const overlaps = (e: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }) =>
301	      this.x < e.x + e.w && this.x + this.w > e.x && this.y < e.y + e.h && this.y + this.h > e.y;
302	    // 怪物桶（Enemy.hurt 自带 8tick 无敌帧，穿透弹不会同帧连击）
303	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
304	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
305	      if (e.dead || !overlaps(e)) continue;
306	      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {
307	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
308	        game.addDamageNumber(this.x, this.y, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
309	      }
310	      if (!pierce) {
311	        if (st.shatterDust) this.shatter(game);
312	        this.dead = true;
313	        return;
314	      }
315	    }
316	    // 友方弹幕（炮弹族 friendly=true）到此为止：不误伤小动物/城镇 NPC
317	    // （原版 friendly 弹对城镇 NPC 免疫；小动物不在敌怪判定内）
318	    if (st.friendly) return;
319	    // 小动物桶（Enemy 单参 shim / Critter.hurt(game) 皆可，被击即死）
320	    const buckets = (game as unknown as { entities?: { critters: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (g: GameHooks) => boolean }>; npcs: Array<{ x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean }> } }).entities;
321	    if (buckets) {
322	      for (const c of buckets.critters) {
323	        if (c.dead || !overlaps(c)) continue;
324	        c.hurt(game);
325	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
326	      }
327	      // 城镇 NPC（TownNPC.hurt：防御减伤+无敌帧+死亡移除）
328	      for (const n of buckets.npcs) {
329	        if (n.dead || !overlaps(n)) continue;
330	        n.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game);
331	        if (!pierce) { this.dead = true; return; }
332	      }
333	    }
334	  }
335	
336	  /** 雪球碎裂（Projectile.Kill type166，Projectile.cs:71758-71767：尘 76 十粒 + Item51） */
337	  private shatter(game: GameHooks): void {
338	    game.spawnParticles(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h / 2, '#F2F8FF', 10, 1, { life: 20, damp: 0.94, grav: -0.01 });
339	    game.playSfx('shatter', 1, this.x, this.y);
340	  }
341	
342	  /** 落点爆炸（Projectile.Kill：type162=Projectile.cs:72768-72831 / type281=72704-72758）：
343	   *  纯范围伤害（162/281 均不在 ExplodeTiles 判定表 Projectile.cs:75260 → 不破坏地形）；
344	   *  原版两段伤害盒 162 为 64×64→144×144、281 为 128×128，此处按 blast 半宽合并一段；
345	   *  friendly 弹不伤玩家（hostile 伤害仅在 getGoodWorld 下对玩家生效，Main.getGoodWorld） */
346	  private blastKill(game: GameHooks): void {
347	    this.dead = true;
348	    const st = TRAP_SHOT_STYLE[this.kind];
349	    const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
350	    game.playSfx('explosion', 1, cx, cy); // SoundID.Item14（Projectile.cs:72769/72706）
351	    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFA050', 14, 3.5, { life: 18, damp: 0.93, grav: -0.02 });
352	    game.spawnParticles(cx, cy, '#FFF0C0', 8, 4.5, { life: 10, damp: 0.9, grav: -0.05 });
353	    this.blastDamage(game, cx, cy, st.blast!);
354	  }
355	
356	  /** 伤害盒结算（炮弹族与烟花火箭共用的 Damage() 语义：friendly 只伤敌怪） */
357	  private blastDamage(game: GameHooks, cx: number, cy: number, r: number): void {
358	    const box = { x: cx - r, y: cy - r, w: 2 * r, h: 2 * r };
359	    for (const ent of game.enemies()) {
360	      const e = ent as unknown as { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; dead: boolean; hurt: (d: number, kx: number, ky: number, g: GameHooks) => boolean; def?: { hitSound?: string[] } };
361	      if (e.dead) continue;
362	      if (!(box.x < e.x + e.w && box.x + box.w > e.x && box.y < e.y + e.h && box.y + box.h > e.y)) continue;
363	      if (e.hurt(this.damage, Math.sign(this.vx) * 2, 0, game)) {
364	        playEnemyHitSound(game, e);
365	        game.addDamageNumber(cx, cy, this.damage, false, '#FFD060');
366	      }
367	    }
368	  }
369	
370	  // ============ 烟花三件套 + 马桶水花（Projectile 167-170 / 415-422 / 733）============
371	
372	  /** 烟花族 AI 主循环。
373	   *  火箭（aiStyle34，Projectile.cs:25677-25739）：无重力直线上升 + 尾烟尘 6，
374	   *  timeLeft 45 到点或撞块 → Kill 爆花；
375	   *  喷泉（aiStyle73，:29369-29510）：静态发射器，锚点 338 消失即 Kill，每 tick 2 粒火花；
376	   *  马桶（aiStyle153，:49278-49314）：11 帧水花后自杀 */
377	  private fireworkUpdate(game: GameHooks): void {
378	    const isRocket = this.kind === 'fireworkRocket' || this.kind === 'fireworksBoxRocket';
379	    if (--this.life <= 0) {
380	      if (isRocket) this.explodeFirework(game); // timeLeft 45 到点 → Projectile.Kill（:73435）
381	      else this.dead = true;                    // 喷泉 3600 自然耗尽（马桶由下方 age 分支自杀）
382	      return;
383	    }
384	    this.age++;
385	    const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
386	    if (isRocket) {
387	      this.x += this.vx;
388	      this.y += this.vy;
389	      // 尾烟（AI_034 :25724-25739）：167-170 每帧 1 粒尘 6；415-418 自 ai[0]>4 起（:25722-25733）
390	      //（引擎粒子 2 tick 一发节流，视觉近似）
391	      const trailOk = this.kind === 'fireworkRocket' || this.age > 4;
392	      if (trailOk && (this.life & 1) === 0) {
393	        game.spawnParticles(this.x + this.w / 2, this.y + this.h, '#A8A8A0', 1, 0.6,
394	          { life: 18, damp: 0.95, grav: -0.02 });
395	      }
396	      // tileCollide=true → 撞实心块同样 Kill 爆花
397	      const tx = Math.floor(cx / TILE), ty = Math.floor(cy / TILE);
398	      if (game.world.store.isSolid(tx, ty)) this.explodeFirework(game);
399	      return;
400	    }
401	    if (this.kind === 'fireworkFountain') {
402	      // 锚点 tile 必须仍是 338（AI_073 :29371-29377，否则 Kill）
403	      const T338 = TILE_BY_KEY['v_338_fireworkfountain'] ?? -1;
404	      const st = game.world.store;
405	      if (!st.inBounds(this.ax, this.ay) || st.type[st.idx(this.ax, this.ay)] !== T338) {
406	        this.dead = true;
407	        return;
408	      }
409	      // num625 = 2 * min(1, timeLeft/60)（:29378-29383）——最后 60t 出速衰减
410	      const sp = 2 * Math.min(1, this.life / 60);
411	      const burst = game.spawnBurst;
412	      if (!burst) return;
413	      if (this.form === 0) {
414	        // 419 黄（:29385-29406）：尘 222，±PI/4 散布，vy×2
415	        burst(cx, cy, FW_DUST.d222_yellow, 2, sp, -Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 4, { yMul: 2, life: 30, damp: 0.96 });
416	      } else if (this.form === 1) {
417	        // 420 红（:29408-29431）：尘 219（每粒 1/5 → 222，此处逐帧取色近似），vx×0.5 vy×2
418	        burst(cx, cy, Math.random() < 0.2 ? FW_DUST.d222_yellow : FW_DUST.d219_red, 2, sp,
419	          -Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 4, { xMul: 0.5, yMul: 2, life: 30, damp: 0.96 });
420	      } else if (this.form === 2) {
421	        // 421 蓝（:29433-29452）：尘 221，±PI/8 窄散布，vy×2.5
422	        burst(cx, cy, FW_DUST.d221_blue, 2, sp, -Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 8, { yMul: 2.5, life: 30, damp: 0.96 });
423	        // timeLeft%10==0 再补 9 粒尘 222 扇面（:29455-29475：x=(i-4)/5 均布）
424	        if (this.life % 10 === 0) {
425	          burst(cx, cy, FW_DUST.d222_yellow, 9, sp * (0.85 + Math.random() * 0.2),
426	            -Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 5, { yMul: 2, life: 30, damp: 0.96 });
427	        }
428	      } else {
429	        // 422 彩虹（:29494-29510）：尘 219 + rand(5)，±PI/4，vy×2
430	        burst(cx, cy, FW_RAINBOW[Math.floor(Math.random() * FW_RAINBOW.length)]!, 2, sp,
431	          -Math.PI / 2, Math.PI / 4, { yMul: 2, life: 30, damp: 0.96 });
432	      }
433	      return;
434	    }
435	    // ---- 马桶水花 733（AI_153_ToiletPop :49278-29314）----
436	    // age 1 ↔ 原版 ai[0]==0（首帧 6 尘 216 + 1 gore）；age 2..11 ↔ ai[0] 1..10（2 尘 + 1/5 gore）；
437	    // age 12 ↔ ai[0]=11 > 10 → Kill。尘向上（speedY -2，velX×0.5，:49299-49305）；
438	    // gore 580-582（:49308-49312）以 2 粒亮水珠近似
439	    if (this.age > 11) {
440	      this.dead = true;
441	      return;
442	    }
443	    const burst = game.spawnBurst;
444	    if (!burst) return;
445	    if (this.age === 1) {
446	      burst(cx, cy, FW_DUST.d216_water, 6, 2, -Math.PI / 2, 0.9, { xMul: 0.5, life: 26, damp: 0.96 });
447	      burst(cx, cy, '#D8ECFA', 2, 2.5, -Math.PI / 2, 0.7, { life: 32, damp: 0.97 });
448	    } else {
449	      burst(cx, cy, FW_DUST.d216_water, 2, 2, -Math.PI / 2, 0.9, { xMul: 0.5, life: 26, damp: 0.96 });
450	      if (Math.random() < 0.2) {
451	        burst(cx, cy, '#D8ECFA', 2, 2.5, -Math.PI / 2, 0.7, { life: 32, damp: 0.97 });
452	      }
453	    }
454	  }
455	
456	  /** 烟花爆花（Projectile.Kill aiStyle==34，Projectile.cs:73435-73921）：
457	   *  SoundID.Item14 + 按弹种彩色尘（数量/速度档 1:1）+ 192×192 伤害盒 Damage()（:73912-73920）。
458	   *  尘速档 = 原版各循环的径向速度（尘自带 0.5 系数初速并入档位）；noGravity 尘按
459	   *  Dust.cs:2227-2230 velocity*=0.93 / fadeIn 缓胀 → damp 0.93 + 45t 存活近似 */
460	  private explodeFirework(game: GameHooks): void {
461	    this.dead = true;
462	    const cx = this.x + this.w / 2, cy = this.y + this.h / 2;
463	    game.playSfx('explosion', 1, cx, cy); // SoundID.Item14（:73437）
464	    const burst = game.spawnBurst
465	      ?? ((x: number, y: number, c: string, n: number, s: number) => game.spawnParticles(x, y, c, n, s));
466	    /** 全向径向爆花（dir=0/spread=PI 覆盖全圆；原版尘方向为随机角，整体旋转不变） */
467	    const radial = (color: string, count: number, speed: number,
468	      opts?: { xMul?: number; yMul?: number }) =>
469	      burst(cx, cy, color, count, speed, 0, Math.PI, { life: 45, damp: 0.93, ...opts });
470	    const rand = (lo: number, hi: number) => lo + Math.floor(Math.random() * (hi - lo));
471	    if (this.kind === 'fireworkRocket') {
472	      // 167-170（:73439-73661）
473	      if (this.form === 0) {
474	        // 红 167（:73439-73473）：400 尘 130，四档速度 4/8/12/16 各 100
475	        radial(FW_DUST.d130_red, 100, 4); radial(FW_DUST.d130_red, 100, 8);
476	        radial(FW_DUST.d130_red, 100, 12); radial(FW_DUST.d130_red, 100, 16);
477	      } else if (this.form === 1) {
478	        // 绿 168（:73475-73518）：400 尘 131；0-100 档 0→2 渐增（取均值 1）、
479	        // 101-250 档 10（150 粒）、251-399 档 13 且 x×1.25/y×0.75（149 粒）
480	        radial(FW_DUST.d131_green, 101, 1); radial(FW_DUST.d131_green, 150, 10);
481	        radial(FW_DUST.d131_green, 149, 13, { xMul: 1.25, yMul: 0.75 });
482	      } else if (this.form === 2) {
483	        // 蓝 169（:73520-73612）：双圈 Lerp 曲线（各 2×num843×20 尘 133）+ 中心 100 尘 132；
484	        // num843 = rand(5,9) 臂、num844 = rand(12,17) 外速、value10 = rand(3,7) 内速
485	        const arms = rand(5, 9), outer = rand(12, 17), inner = rand(3, 7);
486	        radial(FW_DUST.d133_yellow, 80 * arms, outer);
487	        radial(FW_DUST.d133_yellow, 80 * arms, outer / 2);
488	        radial(FW_DUST.d132_blue, 100, outer);
489	      } else {
490	        // 黄 170（:73614-73661）：400 尘 133/134 交替，四档 16/11/8/5 各 100，轴压 0.7 交替
491	        radial(FW_DUST.d133_yellow, 100, 16, { yMul: 0.7 }); radial(FW_DUST.d134_gold, 100, 11, { xMul: 0.7 });
492	        radial(FW_DUST.d133_yellow, 100, 8, { yMul: 0.7 }); radial(FW_DUST.d134_gold, 100, 5, { xMul: 0.7 });
493	      }
494	    } else {
495	      // 415-418 烟花盒（:73663-73898）
496	      if (this.form === 0) {
497	        // 红 415（:73663-73710）：30×num868 臂尘 130（速度 num870→num869 渐增，取均值）+
498	        // 100 尘 130 补充；num868 = rand(5,9)、num869 = rand(10,15)×0.66、num870 = rand(4,7)/2
499	        const arms = rand(5, 9), sp = rand(10, 15) * 0.66, sp0 = rand(4, 7) / 2;
500	        radial(FW_DUST.d130_red, 30 * arms, (sp0 + sp) / 2);
501	        radial(FW_DUST.d130_red, 100, sp);
502	      } else if (this.form === 1) {
503	        // 绿 416（:73712-73760）：num883=20 步 × num882 = rand(3,6)×2 臂螺旋尘 131，
504	        // 速度 1→8 渐增（取均值 4.5），奇偶臂 scale 0.9
505	        radial(FW_DUST.d131_green, 20 * rand(3, 6) * 2, 4.5);
506	      } else if (this.form === 2) {
507	        // 蓝 417（:73762-73801）：150 尘三段（各 50）132/133/132，
508	        // 外圈 num894 = max(rand(4,7),rand(4,7))（x×0.5 旋转）、中段 num893（y×0.5 旋转）、内圈 num892
509	        const sp2 = 4 + Math.random() * 3, sp1 = 4 + Math.random() * 3;
510	        radial(FW_DUST.d132_blue, 50, Math.max(sp1, sp2), { xMul: 0.5 });
511	        radial(FW_DUST.d133_yellow, 50, Math.min(sp1, sp2), { yMul: 0.5 });
512	        radial(FW_DUST.d132_blue, 50, Math.min(sp1, sp2));
513	      } else {
514	        // 黄 418（:73803-73898）：2×num899×15 臂尘 134（SmoothStep num900→num901）+
515	        // 120 补充尘（前 80 尘 133 慢速、后 40 尘 131 外速）
516	        const arms = rand(5, 12), sp = rand(9, 14) * 0.66, sp0 = rand(2, 4) * 0.66;
517	        radial(FW_DUST.d134_gold, 2 * arms * 15, (sp + sp0) / 2);
518	        radial(FW_DUST.d133_yellow, 80, sp0);
519	        radial(FW_DUST.d131_green, 40, sp);
520	      }
521	    }
522	    // 192×192 伤害盒 Damage()（:73912-73920）：烟花火箭 damage 150 结算，
523	    // 烟花盒 damage 0 = 纯视觉（hurt(0) 无意义，直接跳过）
524	    if (this.damage > 0) this.blastDamage(game, cx, cy, 96);
525	  }
526	}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T18:39:56.789Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Now I understand CanHit semantics: a tile-by-tile Bresenham-ish walk (step along the dominant axis one tile at a time):
- If moving horizontally (num5 > num6): step X, then check the tile above (num2-1) and below (num2+1): if BOTH are fully solid (non-slope, non-halfbrick, non-solidtop), the path is squeezed → false (blocked). This models walking through a 1-tile-high gap — you can pass only if at least one of the adjacent vertical tiles is passable (half-brick/slope/platform counts as passable).
- Else step Y and check left/right neighbors similarly.
- The do-while loop: continues while the current tile (num,num2) is inactive, not active, non-solid, or solid-top; returns false when the current tile itself is solid and not solid-top.

Wait, careful with the loop: `do { ...body...; if (tile[num,num2] == null) return false; } while (tile.inactive || !active || !solid || solidTop); return false;`

So the flow: at the end of each iteration, the loop condition is evaluated on the current tile. If the condition is TRUE (tile is passable/non-blocking), loop again (continue walking). If FALSE (current tile is a full solid), exit loop → return false (blocked).

So CanHit returns true ONLY when reaching the target tile. A full-solid tile at the step position blocks. Half-bricks, slopes, platforms (solidTop) don't block; and the side-check ensures a 1-tile gap squeezed between two full solids blocks.

Also `Main.tile[...] == null → return false` — in our store, tiles always exist, so that check maps to inBounds.

Now our TileStore: does it have slope, half, actuated (inactive) data, solidTop? Earlier code referenced `st.half[st.idx(x,y)]` (half-brick) and `st.isSolid(x,y)`. Let me inspect TileStore's data arrays and helpers.
```

27	  // tile 变更监听（chunk 缓存 / 光照脏标记 / 液体模拟统一从这里拿事件）
28	  private listeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
29	
30	  // ---- 多人联机上报钩子（ClientNet 注入；单机为 null 零开销） ----
31	  /** 本地 tile 变更上报（协议层 TileOp 收集器）。liquid 通道不报（客户端本地模拟） */
32	  netReporter: ((op: import('../net/protocol').TileOp) => void) | null = null;
33	  /** 应用远端操作期间置位：上报钩子静默（防回环） */
34	  netSuppress = false;
35	
36	  constructor(w: number, h: number, bufs?: import('../workers/protocol').TileBuffers) {
37	    this.w = w; this.h = h;
38	    // buffers 注入（worker transfer 重建路径）：直接以移交的 ArrayBuffer 包装 TypedArray，
39	    // 跳过分配与拷贝。缺省 explored 由调用方（World.fromPacket）补齐
40	    if (bufs) {
41	      this.type = new Uint16Array(bufs.type);
42	      this.flags = new Uint8Array(bufs.flags);
43	      this.frameX = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameX);
44	      this.frameY = new Uint16Array(bufs.frameY);
45	      this.wall = new Uint16Array(bufs.wall);
46	      this.liquid = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquid);
47	      this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(bufs.liquidType);
48	      this.half = new Uint8Array(bufs.half);
49	      this.slope = new Uint8Array(bufs.slope);
50	      this.wire = new Uint8Array(bufs.wire);
51	      return;
52	    }
53	    const n = w * h;
54	    this.type = new Uint16Array(n);
55	    this.flags = new Uint8Array(n);
56	    this.frameX = new Uint16Array(n);
57	    this.frameY = new Uint16Array(n);
58	    this.wall = new Uint16Array(n);
59	    this.liquid = new Uint8Array(n);
60	    this.liquidType = new Uint8Array(n);
61	    this.half = new Uint8Array(n);
62	    this.slope = new Uint8Array(n);
63	    this.wire = new Uint8Array(n);
64	  }
65	
66	  idx(x: number, y: number): number { return y * this.w + x; }
67	
68	  inBounds(x: number, y: number): boolean {
69	    return x >= 0 && y >= 0 && x < this.w && y < this.h;
70	  }
71	
72	  get(x: number, y: number): number { return this.inBounds(x, y) ? this.type[this.idx(x, y)] : 0; }
73	
74	  isActive(x: number, y: number): boolean {
75	    // 原版 nactive() = active && !inActive（Tile.cs:383-394）：已致动格视为不活跃
76	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && this.type[this.idx(x, y)] !== 0
77	      && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) === 0;
78	  }
79	
80	  /** 原版 inActive()：致动器翻转后的"缩入地下"态（有 type 但不碰撞不阻挡） */
81	  isActuated(x: number, y: number): boolean {
82	    return this.inBounds(x, y) && (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) !== 0;
83	  }
84	
85	  isSolid(x: number, y: number): boolean {
86	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false; // 世界外视为空（边缘自由）
87	    if (this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] & TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) return false; // 已致动不碰撞
88	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
89	    return !!d && d.solid;
90	  }
91	
92	  isPlatform(x: number, y: number): boolean {
93	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
94	    const d = TILE_DEFS[this.type[this.idx(x, y)]];
95	    return !!d && d.platform;
96	  }
97	
98	  onTileChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
99	    this.listeners.push(fn);
100	  }
101	
102	  /** 液体变化独立通道：chunk 缓存不订阅（水不入烘焙画布，无需重建），
103	   *  只有光照订阅（水的光衰减）——避免水流时每帧冲爆 chunk 重建队列造成卡顿 */
104	  private liquidListeners: Array<(x: number, y: number) => void> = [];
105	  onLiquidChanged(fn: (x: number, y: number) => void) {
106	    this.liquidListeners.push(fn);
107	  }
108	
109	  /** 唯一的写入入口（生成/导入期可绕过用 setTileSilent） */
110	  setTile(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
111	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
112	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
113	    this.type[i] = type;
114	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
115	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
116	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
117	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除清半砖/坡面
118	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetTile, x, y, v: type, fx: frameX, fy: frameY });
119	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
120	  }
121	
122	  /** 运行期改半砖/坡面位（锤子交互用；生成期直接写数组即可） */
123	  setHalfBrick(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
124	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
125	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
126	    this.half[i] = v ? 1 : 0;
127	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetHalfBrick, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
128	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
129	  }
130	
131	  setSlope(x: number, y: number, s: number) {
132	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
133	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
134	    this.slope[i] = s;
135	    this.half[i] = 0; // 原版 SlopeTile 无条件 halfBrick(false)（WorldGen.cs:49174），slope=0 复位时同样
136	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetSlope, x, y, v: s, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
137	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
138	  }
139	
140	  setTileSilent(x: number, y: number, type: number, frameX = 0, frameY = 0) {
141	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
142	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
143	    this.type[i] = type;
144	    this.flags[i] = type !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
145	    this.frameX[i] = frameX;
146	    this.frameY[i] = frameY;
147	    if (type === 0) { this.half[i] = 0; this.slope[i] = 0; } // 挖除同步清位（防生成期脏残留）
148	  }
149	
150	  setWall(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
151	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
152	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
153	    if (this.wall[i] !== wall) {
154	      this.wall[i] = wall;
155	      if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWall, x, y, v: wall, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
156	      this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
157	    }
158	  }
159	
160	  setWallSilent(x: number, y: number, wall: number) {
161	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
162	    this.wall[this.idx(x, y)] = wall;
163	  }
164	
165	  /** 导线/致动位写入（Wiring 系统）。不发 listeners：导线不进 chunk 烘焙画布，
166	   *  走 Renderer 动态覆盖层；致动位变化才需要标脏 chunk（调用方负责） */
167	  setWire(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
168	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
169	    this.wire[this.idx(x, y)] = bits & 63;
170	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetWire, x, y, v: bits & 63, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
171	  }
172	
173	  setWireSilent(x: number, y: number, bits: number) {
174	    this.setWire(x, y, bits);
175	  }
176	
177	  /** 致动器翻转 inActive 态（Wiring.DeActive/ReActive 用）：影响碰撞与渲染，须发 listeners */
178	  setActuated(x: number, y: number, v: boolean) {
179	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
180	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
181	    const b = v ? (this.wire[i] | TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED) : (this.wire[i] & ~TileStore.WIRE_ACTUATED);
182	    if (this.wire[i] === b) return;
183	    this.wire[i] = b;
184	    if (this.netReporter && !this.netSuppress) this.netReporter({ a: TileOpAction.SetActuated, x, y, v: v ? 1 : 0, fx: 0, fy: 0 });
185	    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
186	  }
187	
188	  setLiquid(x: number, y: number, amount: number, type: number) {
189	    if (!this.inBounds(x, y)) return;
190	    const i = this.idx(x, y);
191	    const a = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, amount | 0));
192	    if (this.liquid[i] === a && this.liquidType[i] === type) return;
193	    this.liquid[i] = a;
194	    this.liquidType[i] = type;
195	    this.liquidListeners.forEach((fn) => fn(x, y));
196	  }
197	}
198	

```

1	// 分轴扫描 tile AABB 碰撞
2	import { TILE } from '../core/constants';
3	import type { World } from '../world/World';
4	
5	export interface Body {
6	  x: number; y: number;       // 左上角（px）
7	  w: number; h: number;
8	  vx: number; vy: number;
9	  onGround: boolean;
10	  hitWall: boolean;
11	  hitHead: boolean;
12	  /** 平台单向碰撞：仅当开启且下落时与平台碰撞 */
13	  dropThrough?: boolean;
14	  /** 启用原版自动上台阶（Collision.StepUp）：行走实体（玩家/敌人/小动物/城镇NPC）置 true；
15	   *  掉落物/弹幕/墓碑等不参与（原版它们不调 StepUp） */
16	  stepUp?: boolean;
17	}
18	
19	/** Collision.StepUp（Collision.cs:3641-3770，gravDir=1、holdsMatching=false）：
20	 *  水平行走【意图速度】先探前方一列 → 满足净空门即抬升最多 16.1px
21	 *  （半砖 8px / 整砖台阶 16px）。玩家（Player.cs:23258/:27753）与全部 NPC
22	 *  （NPC.cs:54382，先于 TileCollision 用未受阻的速度调用）共用——
23	 *  此前只有玩家有自研版上台阶，敌人/小动物/城镇 NPC 全部卡死在半砖/台阶。
24	 *  gfxOffY 视觉缓动无对应渲染通道，位置直接抬升（原版 NPC 同样瞬间抬）。 */
25	function applyStepUp(b: Body, world: World): void {
26	  const vx = b.vx;
27	  if (vx === 0) return;
28	  const dir = Math.sign(vx);
29	  const st = world.store;
30	  const probeX = b.x + vx;                       // vector.X = position.X + velocity.X
31	  const num2 = Math.floor((probeX + b.w / 2 + (b.w / 2 + 1) * dir) / TILE);
32	  const num3 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 1) / TILE); // 脚底行（gravDir=1）
33	  const num4 = Math.floor(b.h / TILE) + (b.h % TILE !== 0 ? 1 : 0);
34	  if (!st.inBounds(num2, num3) || num3 >= st.h - 40) return;
35	  const solidAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
36	    st.inBounds(x, y) && st.isSolid(x, y);       // 平台非 tileSolid → 不阻挡（vanilla 语义）
37	  const halfAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean =>
38	    st.inBounds(x, y) && !!st.half[st.idx(x, y)];
39	  const slopeAt = (x: number, y: number): number =>
40	    st.inBounds(x, y) ? st.slope[st.idx(x, y)] : 0;
41	  // flag（:3700-3708）：身体放入探柱（j=2..num4 上方行全净空）
42	  for (let j = 2; j <= num4; j++) if (solidAt(num2, num3 - j)) return;
43	  // flag2（:3710-3714）：后上方对角净空
44	  if (solidAt(num2 - dir, num3 - num4)) return;
45	  const centerX = b.x + b.w / 2;
46	  // flag3（:3725-3728）：脚上一格净空 / 面朝坡 / 半砖且其上净空
47	  {
48	    const s = slopeAt(num2, num3 - 1);
49	    const ok = !solidAt(num2, num3 - 1)
50	      || (s === 1 && centerX > num2 * TILE)
51	      || (s === 2 && centerX < num2 * TILE + TILE)
52	      || (halfAt(num2, num3 - 1) && !solidAt(num2, num3 - num4 - 1));
53	    if (!ok) return;
54	  }
55	  // flag4（:3730-3737）：落脚格实心（面朝坡且身体沉入）/ 或脚上一格是半砖
56	  {
57	    const fs = slopeAt(num2, num3);
58	    const topSlope = fs === 1 || fs === 2;
59	    const a = solidAt(num2, num3)
60	      && (!topSlope || (fs === 1 && centerX < num2 * TILE) || (fs === 2 && centerX > num2 * TILE + TILE))
61	      && (!topSlope || b.y + b.h > num3 * TILE);
62	    const bb = halfAt(num2, num3 - 1) && solidAt(num2, num3 - 1);
63	    if (!(a || bb)) return;
64	  }
65	  // X 重叠门（:3745-3748）：探柱与移动后身体横向相交
66	  if (!(num2 * TILE < probeX + b.w && num2 * TILE + TILE > probeX)) return;
67	  // 抬升（:3750-3770）：半砖上一格 → rowTop-8；本格半砖 → rowTop+8；上限 16.1px
68	  let target = num3 * TILE;
69	  if (halfAt(num2, num3 - 1)) target -= 8;
70	  else if (halfAt(num2, num3)) target += 8;
71	  if (target >= b.y + b.h) return;
72	  if (b.y + b.h - target > 16.1) return;
73	  b.y = target - b.h;
74	}
75	
76	/** 格子的半砖碰撞盒（原版 Collision.cs:1320-1324 三处一致）：下半 8px。
77	 *  返回 null = 非实心；[top, bottom] = 碰撞盒的像素 y 区间 */
78	function solidSpan(world: World, tx: number, ty: number): [number, number] | null {
79	  const st = world.store;
80	  if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty)) return null;
81	  const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[st.idx(tx, ty)] ? 8 : 0);
82	  return [top, (ty + 1) * TILE];
83	}
84	
85	/** Collision.StepDown（Collision.cs:3577-3638，gravDir=1、waterWalk=false）：
86	 *  贴地行走时脚下 7~17px 内有落面（半砖顶/台阶/平台）→ 直接吸附下去，
87	 *  消除下楼梯的腾空帧（onGround 连续，AI 跳跃/攻击门不抖动）。
88	 *  玩家（Player.cs:23252，vy==gravity 时）与 NPC（NPC.cs:54377，vy==0 时）共用。 */
89	function applyStepDown(b: Body, world: World): void {
90	  const vx = b.vx;
91	  if (vx === 0) return;
92	  const st = world.store;
93	  const probeX = b.x + vx;                          // vector.X = position.X + velocity.X
94	  // vector.Y = ⌊(y+h)/16⌋×16 - h（:3581 先把脚底吸附到格线）
95	  const snapY = Math.floor((b.y + b.h) / TILE) * TILE - b.h;
96	  const rowA = Math.floor((snapY + b.h + 4) / TILE); // num3（:3585）
97	  const col0 = Math.floor(probeX / TILE), col1 = Math.floor((probeX + b.w) / TILE);
98	  const num4 = Math.floor(b.h / TILE) + (b.h % TILE !== 0 ? 1 : 0);
99	  let best = (rowA + num4) * TILE;                  // num5 初始（远下方默认）
100	  for (let i = col0; i <= col1; i++) {
101	    for (let j = rowA; j <= rowA + 1; j++) {
102	      if (!st.inBounds(i, j)) continue;
103	      const ji = st.idx(i, j);
104	      if (!(st.isSolid(i, j) || st.isPlatform(i, j))) continue; // tileSolid||tileSolidTop（:3614）
105	      let top = j * TILE;
106	      if (st.half[ji]) top += 8;                    // 半砖顶 +8（:3618-3621）
107	      // FloatIntersect(tile 行, 以原 position 判定 :3623)
108	      if (i * TILE < b.x + b.w && i * TILE + TILE > b.x
109	        && j * TILE - 17 < b.y + b.h && j * TILE - 17 + TILE > b.y && top < best) {
110	        best = top;
111	      }
112	    }
113	  }
114	  const gap = best - (b.y + b.h);                   // num10（:3630）
115	  if (gap > 7 && gap < 17) b.y = best - b.h;        // :3632-3637
116	}
117	
118	/** 单个轴的移动 + 碰撞解析。返回是否发生碰撞。 */
119	function moveAxis(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number): { hitX: boolean; hitY: boolean } {
120	  let hitX = false, hitY = false;
121	  const st = world.store;
122	  const avx = Math.abs(b.vx);
123	  // 原版坡面放行门（Collision.cs:2361-2387 flag3）：从高/低侧贴面走近的坡面格
124	  // 不参与本轴碰撞（交给 slopeCollide 对角线贴合）。全部以【移动前】位置判定
125	  // （原版 vector3,:2306）——传参 ox/oy,勿用移动后的 b.x/b.y。
126	  // 地面坡门是 feet-|vx| <= 格底(top+16,:2375/:2379 的 num7=格高)——不是格顶!
127	  // 写成格顶会让低侧贴地进入永远不过 → 孤立坡前一格被 X 拦截卡死。
128	  // slope1 左高右低 / 2 右高左低 / 3 左低右高(天花板) / 4 右低左高(天花板)
129	  const slopePass = (tx: number, ty: number, ox: number, oy: number): boolean => {
130	    const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
131	    const sl = st.slope[j];
132	    if (sl === 0 || st.half[j]) return false;
133	    const top = ty * TILE;
134	    if (sl === 1) return oy + b.h - avx <= top + TILE && ox >= tx * TILE;
135	    if (sl === 2) return oy + b.h - avx <= top + TILE && ox + b.w <= tx * TILE + TILE;
136	    if (sl === 3) return oy + avx >= top && ox >= tx * TILE;
137	    return oy + avx >= top && ox + b.w <= tx * TILE + TILE; // 4
138	  };
139	  // X 轴
140	  if (dx !== 0) {
141	    b.x += dx;
142	    const dir = Math.sign(dx);
143	    const oldX = b.x - dx; // 本步移动前位置（原版 vector3）
144	    const edgeX = dir > 0 ? b.x + b.w : b.x;
145	    const tx = Math.floor(edgeX / TILE);
146	    const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
147	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
148	      if (slopePass(tx, ty, oldX, b.y)) continue;
149	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
150	      // 半砖只占下半：身体底部没超过半砖顶面（ty*16+8）则不拦
151	      if (span && b.y + b.h > span[0]) {
152	        // 原版拦截前提（:2406/:2426）：上一位置【完全】在该格一侧（贴面接近）才拦；
153	        // 身体已横向跨在格上（上坡爬升中段,脚沉在斜面下）不拦——交给 slopeCollide
154	        // 抬升,否则上坡会被每帧推回卡死
155	        if (dir > 0 ? oldX + b.w > tx * TILE + 0.01 : oldX < (tx + 1) * TILE - 0.01) continue;
156	        // hoik 坡链放行（:2412/:2432）：身后格是配套坡面（同向坡链中段）→ 不拦
157	        const bj = st.idx(tx - Math.sign(dir), ty);
158	        if (dir > 0 && (st.slope[bj] === 2 || st.slope[bj] === 4)) continue;
159	        if (dir < 0 && (st.slope[bj] === 1 || st.slope[bj] === 3)) continue;
160	        if (dir > 0) b.x = tx * TILE - b.w;
161	        else b.x = (tx + 1) * TILE;
162	        b.vx = 0;
163	        hitX = true;
164	        break;
165	      }
166	    }
167	  }
168	  // Y 轴
169	  if (dy !== 0) {
170	    const oldY = b.y; // 移动前位置（原版 vector3.Y）
171	    b.y += dy;
172	    const dir = Math.sign(dy);
173	    const edgeY = dir > 0 ? b.y + b.h : b.y;
174	    const ty = Math.floor(edgeY / TILE);
175	    const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
176	    // 原版取整行最高面（Collision.cs:1610-1631 num13 取最大盒顶），不能 break 在最左列——
177	    // 否则左列半砖/右列整砖时身体会嵌进整砖 8px
178	    let bestTop = Infinity;
179	    for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
180	      const solid = world.store.isSolid(tx, ty);
181	      const span = solidSpan(world, tx, ty);
182	      const plat = !b.dropThrough && dir > 0 && world.store.isPlatform(tx, ty)
183	        && (b.y + b.h) - dy <= ty * TILE + 1; // 上一位置在平台之上
184	      if (dir > 0) {
185	        if (!solid && !plat) continue;
186	        if (slopePass(tx, ty, b.x, oldY)) continue; // 坡面格交给 slopeCollide 对角线贴合
187	        // 落地门槛（Collision.cs:1610/1631）：新底部越过盒顶 且 上一位置在盒顶之上，
188	        // 否则（嵌入/侧入）不吸附——半砖盒顶是 ty*16+8，只进入行上半不算落地
189	        const top = span ? span[0] : ty * TILE;
190	        if (b.y + b.h <= top || (b.y + b.h) - dy > top + 0.01) continue;
191	        if (top < bestTop) bestTop = top;
192	      } else {
193	        if (!solid) continue;
194	        if (slopePass(tx, ty, b.x, oldY)) continue;
195	        // 上顶：上一位置在盒底之下才命中；取最低盒底（最先撞到的天花板）
196	        const bottom = (ty + 1) * TILE;
197	        if (b.y >= bottom || b.y - dy < bottom - 0.01) continue;
198	        if (bottom < bestTop) bestTop = bottom;
199	      }
200	    }
201	    if (bestTop !== Infinity) {
202	      if (dir > 0) { b.y = bestTop - b.h; b.onGround = true; }
203	      else { b.y = bestTop; b.hitHead = true; }
204	      b.vy = 0;
205	      hitY = true;
206	    }
207	  }
208	  return { hitX, hitY };
209	}
210	
211	/** 原版 Collision.SlopeCollision（1456 Collision.cs:1796-2036）适配：
212	 *  常规碰撞后对重叠坡面格做对角线贴合——身体沉到斜面下时抬回斜面上
213	 *  （走路沿坡爬升;多格取最高贴合位,与原版 y 最小值机制一致）。
214	 *  slope1/2 地面坡:slope1 左高右低(左行爬升)、slope2 右高左低;
215	 *  slope3/4 天花板坡:对称向下推离。
216	 *  受阻回退已对齐原版 :2004-2033（速度清零 + slope 向性 X 补偿）。
217	 *  与原版的偏差（稳定性取舍,均在行内注释标记）：
218	 *  - num4 钳 ≥0（原版 num4<0 跳过）：坡顶过渡瞬间防脱钩下沉
219	 *  - 未移植 :2003 的"重跑 TileCollision 校验"本体（分轴结构代价高,
220	 *    钳位已覆盖其主收益;受阻回退语义由嵌入守卫分支等价提供） */
221	function slopeCollide(b: Body, world: World): void {
222	  const st = world.store;
223	  const x0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), x1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
224	  const y0 = Math.floor(b.y / TILE), y1 = Math.floor((b.y + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
225	  let bestLift = 0;      // 最大抬升量（负 y 位移;0 = 无贴合）
226	  let bestDrop = 0;      // 天花板最大下推量
227	  for (let tx = x0; tx <= x1; tx++) {
228	    for (let ty = y0; ty <= y1; ty++) {
229	      if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
230	      const i = st.idx(tx, ty);
231	      const slope = st.slope[i];
232	      if (slope === 0 || st.half[i] || !st.isSolid(tx, ty)) continue;
233	      const vx0 = tx * TILE, vy0 = ty * TILE;
234	      if (b.x + b.w <= vx0 || b.x >= vx0 + TILE || b.y + b.h <= vy0 || b.y >= vy0 + TILE) continue;
235	      // 对角线水平偏移（:1882-1893/1927-1934）：slope1/3 取身体左缘进格深度,
236	      // slope2/4 取右缘。钳到 ≥0（与原版 num4<0 即跳过的差异,见函数头注释）：
237	      // 坡顶过渡瞬间(高侧缘越过坡面)原版会脱钩,脚底残留在对角线端点下方数像素,
238	      // 落地门槛不补救 → 角色沉进坡格;钳位后贴合保持到水平重叠结束,送脚到
239	      // 对角线高端点(=相邻整砖顶),由常规落地无缝接管
240	      let num4: number;
241	      if (slope === 1 || slope === 3) num4 = Math.max(0, b.x - vx0);
242	      else num4 = Math.max(0, vx0 + TILE - (b.x + b.w));
243	      if (slope === 3 || slope === 4) {
244	        // 天花板坡（:1883-1921）：头在斜面上方才贴合下推
245	        if (b.y <= vy0 + TILE - num4) {
246	          const num5 = vy0 + TILE - b.y - num4;
247	          if (num5 > bestDrop) bestDrop = num5;
248	        }
249	      } else {
250	        // 地面坡（:1935-1966）：脚沉到斜面之下才抬回（num7<0 即抬升）
251	        if (b.y + b.h < vy0 + num4) continue;
252	        const num7 = vy0 - (b.y + b.h) + num4;
253	        if (num7 < bestLift) bestLift = num7;
254	      }
255	    }
256	  }
257	  if (bestLift < 0) {
258	    const ny = b.y + bestLift;
259	    // 抬升后不能嵌进实心
260	    let blocked = false;
261	    const bx0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), bx1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
262	    const by0 = Math.floor(ny / TILE), by1 = Math.floor((ny + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
263	    for (let tx = bx0; tx <= bx1 && !blocked; tx++) {
264	      for (let ty = by0; ty <= by1; ty++) {
265	        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
266	        const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
267	        if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty) || st.slope[j] > 0 || st.half[j]) continue;
268	        const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0);
269	        if (b.x + b.w > tx * TILE && b.x < tx * TILE + TILE && ny + b.h > top && ny < (ty + 1) * TILE) { blocked = true; break; }
270	      }
271	    }
272	    if (!blocked) {
273	      b.y = ny;
274	      if (b.vy > 0) b.vy = 0;
275	      b.onGround = true;
276	    } else {
277	      // 抬升受阻回退（原版 :2004-2018 的速度清零语义）——墙角坡干净挡停而非楔进角落。
278	      // 原版另有按 shortfall 的 X 滑动补偿,但其"重跑校验"保证每帧只作用一次;
279	      // 我们的分轴结构下输入每帧重新加速,X 推会变棘轮,故只取清零、不推 X
280	      b.vx = 0;
281	      b.vy = 0;
282	    }
283	  }
284	  if (bestDrop > 0) {
285	    // 下推守卫:推离后不能嵌进下方实心(对称于抬升守卫)
286	    const ny = b.y + bestDrop;
287	    let blocked = false;
288	    const bx0 = Math.floor(b.x / TILE), bx1 = Math.floor((b.x + b.w - 0.01) / TILE);
289	    const by0 = Math.floor(ny / TILE), by1 = Math.floor((ny + b.h - 0.01) / TILE);
290	    for (let tx = bx0; tx <= bx1 && !blocked; tx++) {
291	      for (let ty = by0; ty <= by1; ty++) {
292	        if (!st.inBounds(tx, ty)) continue;
293	        const j = st.idx(tx, ty);
294	        if (!st.isSolid(tx, ty) || st.slope[j] > 0 || st.half[j]) continue;
295	        const top = ty * TILE + (st.half[j] ? 8 : 0);
296	        if (b.x + b.w > tx * TILE && b.x < tx * TILE + TILE && ny + b.h > top && ny < (ty + 1) * TILE) { blocked = true; break; }
297	      }
298	    }
299	    if (!blocked) {
300	      b.y = ny;
301	      if (b.vy < 0.0101) b.vy = 0.0101; // 原版向下推离（:1902-1905）
302	    } else {
303	      // 天花板坡下推受阻（原版 :2020-2033 镜像）：速度清零（X 补偿同上不取）
304	      b.vx = 0;
305	      b.vy = 0;
306	    }
307	  }
308	}
309	
310	/** 移动并碰撞。高速时分子步防穿透。 */
311	export function moveAndCollide(b: Body, world: World, dx: number, dy: number) {
312	  b.onGround = false;
313	  b.hitWall = false;
314	  b.hitHead = false;
315	  // 原版 StepUp/StepDown 在 TileCollision 之前用未受阻的意图速度探测
316	  // （Player.cs:23252-23263 / NPC.cs:54377-54382）
317	  if (b.stepUp) {
318	    if (b.vy >= 0) applyStepDown(b, world);
319	    applyStepUp(b, world);
320	  }
321	  // 速度辅助（Collision.cs:1773-1790）：脚底中心所在格(原版为整行落地候选格的近似)
322	  // 是 slope1/2、水平速度朝低侧、脚已沉到对角线 → dy += |dx|
323	  // （等价 Velocity.Y += |Velocity.X|）把身体粘到斜面,消除坡顶角/高速下坡 1 帧的格顶滑行
324	  {
325	    const st = world.store;
326	    const feetRow = Math.floor((b.y + b.h) / TILE);
327	    const midCol = Math.floor((b.x + b.w / 2) / TILE);
328	    if (dx !== 0 && st.inBounds(midCol, feetRow)) {
329	      const fi = st.idx(midCol, feetRow);
330	      const fs = st.slope[fi];
331	      if ((fs === 1 || fs === 2) && !st.half[fi] && st.isSolid(midCol, feetRow)) {
332	        const vx0 = midCol * TILE;
333	        const num4 = fs === 1 ? b.x - vx0 : vx0 + TILE - (b.x + b.w);
334	        if (num4 >= 0 && b.y + b.h >= feetRow * TILE + num4
335	          && ((fs === 1 && b.vx > 0) || (fs === 2 && b.vx < 0))) {
336	          dy += Math.abs(dx);
337	        }
338	      }
339	    }
340	  }
341	  const maxStep = 8;
342	  const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(Math.max(Math.abs(dx), Math.abs(dy)) / maxStep));
343	  for (let i = 0; i < steps; i++) {
344	    const sx = dx / steps, sy = dy / steps;
345	    const { hitX, hitY } = moveAxis(b, world, sx, sy);
346	    if (hitX) { b.hitWall = true; dx = 0; }
347	    if (hitY) dy = 0;
348	    if (dx === 0 && dy === 0) break;
349	  }
350	  slopeCollide(b, world);
351	}
352

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T18:40:50.390Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
Confirmed: SlopeCollision has NO velocity decay/friction. It only does slope diagonal adhesion. So the claim in our comment (Enemy.ts:938-939) "原版经由 SlopeCollision 的速度衰减近似" is factually wrong on two counts:
1. SlopeCollision does not decay velocity.
2. The slope collision system IS implemented in our repo (moveAndCollide → slopeCollide).

So what should `if (this.onGround) this.vx *= 0.85` be? In vanilla, NPC ground friction... For fighters, velocity.X is directly driven (accel 0.1, clamp 1.0) each tick, so no friction needed. The 0.85 in our code is likely a hand-tuned damp to prevent sliding. Since AI_003 in our fighterAI already does vx += dir*0.1 and clamps ±1, applying *0.85 on top makes the fighter slower than vanilla (0.85 damp each tick → steady state vx = 0.1*dir/(1-0.85)≈0.67 rather than 1.0).

Hmm wait — where exactly is this friction applied? Line 938-939 is inside fighterAI (after moveAndCollide). So our fighter moves at ~0.67 px/t instead of 1.0. That's a real behavioral deviation. Vanilla fighters walk at 1.0 px/t max.

But careful: the vanilla NPC collision resets velocity.X to 0 when hitting a wall (TileCollision). Our moveAndCollide sets b.vx=0 on hitX. But on flat ground with no wall, vanilla keeps velocity.X and just re-adds 0.1 each tick clamped to 1.0 → walks at exactly 1.0 px/t.

Our fighterAI: `this.vx += dir * 0.1; clamp ±1;` then moveAndCollide, then `if (this.onGround) this.vx *= 0.85;` → next tick vx = 0.85vx+0.1 → steady state 0.667. So our fighters are ~33% slower than vanilla.

Fix per 1:1: remove the friction line, since vanilla AI_003 has no ground friction. But why was it added? Possibly to stop knockback sliding. Knockback: vanilla NPC velocity is overwritten by AI each tick (vx += dir*0.1 clamps at 1.0 — actually that doesn't zero knockback either; knockback in vanilla decays because... hmm, `vx += dir*0.1` then clamp ±1 → knockback vx of 6 would remain 6! But vanilla fighters don't slide forever. Because TileCollision zeroes velocity when colliding... no, on flat ground no collision.

Actually vanilla NPC.knockBackResist reduces knockback, and then velocity.X persists... Hmm, but vanilla AI_003 does have this: let me look for how velocity gets normalized. Let me actually find the core walk code of AI_003 (the num1/num2 0.1/1.0 part).

So the vanilla ground friction is: **when |vx| exceeds max speed AND on ground → velocity *= 0.8** (whole velocity, both axes!). export interface MoonEventState {
  /** 0=无事件 1=霜月 2=南瓜月 */
  kind: 0 | 1 | 2;
  /** 当前波（1..20；20 为终波，lookup[20]=0 不再推进） */
  waveNumber: number;
  /** 当前波已得分（浮点——专家/大师乘区可出 0.5 分） */
  waveKills: number;
  /** 本次事件累计总分（黎明广播 Misc.*MoonScore 用） */
  totalInvasionPoints: number;
}

export function newMoonEventState(): MoonEventState {
  return { kind: 0, waveNumber: 0, waveKills: 0, totalInvasionPoints: 0 };
}

  return w.moonEvent.kind !== 0;
}

targetSeekDir(player: Player | null): number {

}

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-11T18:42:28.718Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

```
I have enough understanding. Now, decision on the confused implementation approach.

Given the architecture, the cleanest faithful implementation with a single choke point: **mirror the player X coordinate** that all AIs use for horizontal targeting. But there are direct uses of player.cx in movement deltas (floatEye dirX, swarmer dx, dungeonSpirit ex...). Vanilla: those AIs use `direction` (from TargetClosest) for X — which IS inverted. And for Y, vanilla's TargetClosest does NOT invert directionY. So in vanilla, float eye confused: dirX inverted, dirY normal. Hmm interesting — so for AI_002 the delta-X is replaced by direction? Let me verify quickly by reading AI_002_FloatingEye targeting: earlier excerpt showed `direction = 1; if (velocity.X > 0) ...` hmm that was for the no-target branch. Let me check how AI_002 uses direction vs direct player position.

					velocity.X = 2f;

```
Confirmed: AI_002 moves by `direction` (inverted by confused) with position gates (`position.X > player.position.X + width` for direction==-1). With confused, direction is inverted, so the eye flies away horizontally while still approaching vertically. Note the position gates mean the acceleration only applies when actually on the far side — combined with inverted direction, the NPC hovers at the boundary. That's the vanilla confused behavior for float eyes: they drift to the side.

Our floatEyeAI uses dirX/dirY directly (a registered simplification of the position-gated accel). With confused, invert dirX only.

**Decision**: implement confused by inverting the horizontal seek direction in each AI's targeting step, mirroring vanilla TargetClosest's `direction *= -1`. Concretely:
1. Add `get confused()`.
2. Add helper `seekDirX(player)` returning the sign with confused inversion.

import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { World } from '../src/world/World';
import { Enemy } from '../src/entities/Enemy';

// AI 家族错误回退消除回归（aiStyle 0/17/19/23/25/39/41/44/56）
type DartLike = { vx: number; vy: number; damage: number };

function flatWorld(playerAt: { x: number; y: number }) {
  const w = new World(80, 80, 42, 'open');
  const st = w.store;
  for (let x = 10; x <= 70; x++) st.setTileSilent(x, 60, 2); // 地板 y=60
  const shots: DartLike[] = [];
  const hooks = {
    world: w,
    player: {
      x: playerAt.x * 16, y: playerAt.y * 16, w: 20, h: 42,
      cx: playerAt.x * 16, cy: playerAt.y * 16 + 21,
      dead: false, useTime: 0,
    },
    playSfx: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {}, spawnParticles: () => {},
    spawnDrop: () => null, damagePlayer: () => {},
    entities: { add: (e: unknown) => { shots.push(e as DartLike); } },
  } as never;
  return { w, hooks, shots };
}

describe('AI 家族回退消除', () => {
  it('被缚NPC 105（aiStyle 0）：原地站立不动、零接触伤害', () => {
    const { hooks } = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 58 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(105, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    expect(e.def.damage).toBe(0);          // friendly 旗 → 零伤害
    const x0 = Math.round(e.x);
    for (let t = 0; t < 120; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(Math.abs(e.x - x0)).toBeLessThan(1); // 不游走
  });

  it('秃鹫 61（aiStyle 17）：栖息不动；玩家进入 200px 盒 → 起飞（vy=-6）', () => {
    const far = flatWorld({ x: 60, y: 40 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(61, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    e.y = 60 * 16 - e.h; e.vy = 0;   // 贴地栖息（悬空出生原版语义=直接起飞）
    for (let t = 0; t < 60; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, far.hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(0);                  // 远处保持栖息
    const near = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 58 });
    const e2 = Enemy.fromVanilla(61, 40 * 16, 59 * 16)!;
    let tookOff = false;
    for (let t = 0; t < 30; t++) {
      e2.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, near.hooks);
      if (e2.ai0 === 1) { tookOff = true; break; }
    }
    expect(tookOff).toBe(true);
  });

  it('蚁狮 69（aiStyle 19）：扎地不动；玩家在上方 200t 后射沙球（速 12、伤 10）', () => {
    const { hooks, shots } = flatWorld({ x: 40, y: 55 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(69, 40 * 16, 58 * 16)!;
    const x0 = Math.round(e.x);
    let fired = false;
    for (let t = 0; t < 400 && !fired; t++) {
      e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
      if (shots.length > 0) fired = true;
    }
    expect(fired).toBe(true);
    expect(Math.abs(e.x - x0)).toBeLessThan(2); // 全程扎根不位移
    expect(shots[0].damage).toBe(10);
    expect(Math.hypot(shots[0].vx, shots[0].vy)).toBeCloseTo(12, 1);
  });

  it('飞行武器 83（aiStyle 23）：三态循环——冲刺(9)→漂移→蓄转(转速递增)', () => {
    const { hooks } = flatWorld({ x: 44, y: 58 });
    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(83, 40 * 16, 58 * 16)!;
    e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(1);                                  // 冲刺后转漂移
    expect(Math.hypot(e.vx, e.vy)).toBeCloseTo(9, 1);
    for (let t = 0; t < 100; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(2);                                  // 漂移 100t → 蓄转
    const ang0 = e.visAngle;
    e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, hooks);
    expect(e.visAngle).not.toBe(ang0);                      // 转速递增旋转中
  });

  it('宝箱怪 85（aiStyle 25）：远处伪装；玩家近 200px → 显形起跳循环', () => {
    const far = flatWorld({ x: 60, y: 50 });

    e.y = 60 * 16 - e.h; e.vy = 0;   // 贴地伪装
    for (let t = 0; t < 90; t++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, far.hooks);
    expect(e.ai0).toBe(0);                                  // 伪装

      if (e2.ai0 === 1) { active = true; break; }
    }
    expect(active).toBe(true);

    const { hooks } = flatWorld({ x: 70, y: 58 });   // >200px 持续蓄力（cs:29344 门），避免走近后停蓄
